      const applyTheme = () => {
        const theme =
          localStorage.getItem("docs-theme") ?? (matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").matches ? "light" : "dark")
        document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme
        document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.setAttribute("content", theme === "light" ? "#ffffff" : "#000000")
      }

      applyTheme()
      document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", applyTheme)
      matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").addEventListener("change", applyTheme)
      document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        if (!(event.target instanceof Element) || !event.target.closest("[data-theme-toggle]")) return
        localStorage.setItem("docs-theme", document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "light" ? "dark" : "light")
        applyTheme()
      })

data-search-input type="search" placeholder="Search docs" aria-label="Search docs" autocomplete="off"><button type="submit" aria-label="Close search">Esc</button></form><div class="search-results" data-search-results><p>Start typing to search the documentation.</p></div></dialog><script>
  let pagefind

  document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if ((event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
      event.preventDefault()
      const dialog = document.querySelector("[data-search-dialog]")
      dialog?.showModal()
      dialog?.querySelector("[data-search-input]")?.focus()
    }
  })

  const setupSearch = () => {
    const dialog = document.querySelector("[data-search-dialog]")
    const input = document.querySelector("[data-search-input]")
    const results = document.querySelector("[data-search-results]")
    const trigger = document.querySelector("[data-search-open]")
    if (trigger && !trigger.dataset.searchReady) {
      trigger.dataset.searchReady = "true"
      trigger.addEventListener("click", () => {
        dialog?.showModal()
        input?.focus()
      })
    }

    if (input && results && !input.dataset.searchReady) input.addEventListener("input", async () => {
      input.dataset.searchReady = "true"
      const query = input.value.trim()
      if (!query) {
        results.innerHTML = "<p>Start typing to search the documentation.</p>"
        return
      }

      const pagefindPath = dialog?.dataset.pagefindPath
      if (!pagefindPath) return
      const api = pagefind ?? (pagefind = await import(pagefindPath))
      const search = await api.search(query)
      const pages = await Promise.all(search.results.slice(0, 8).map((result) => result.data()))
      results.replaceChildren(
        ...pages.map((page) => {
          const link = document.createElement("a")
          const base = dialog.dataset.baseUrl ?? "/"
          link.href = page.url.startsWith(base)
            ? page.url
            : `${dialog.dataset.baseUrl?.replace(/\/$/, "")}${page.url}`
          const title = document.createElement("strong")
          title.textContent = page.meta.title
          const excerpt = document.createElement("span")
          excerpt.innerHTML = page.excerpt
          link.append(title, excerpt)
          return link
        }),
      )
      if (!pages.length) results.innerHTML = "<p>No results found.</p>"
    })
  }

  setupSearch()
  document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setupSearch)
</script><details class="mobile-navigation"><summary>Build navigation</summary><nav class="docs-sidebar" aria-label="Documentation"><section><ul><li><a id="docs-content"><article class="prose" data-pagefind-body data-pagefind-meta="title:Overview"><h1>Overview<button type="button" aria-label="Copy page as Markdown" data-copy-markdown data-pagefind-ignore><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="8" y="8" width="13" height="13" To change the terminal UI, build a <a href="/v2/docs/build/plugins/cli">CLI
plugin</a>.</p>
<p>Migrating an existing OpenCode 1 plugin? Follow the <a href="/v2/docs/build/plugins/migrate-v1">V1 plugin migration guide</a>.</p>

<p>Plugins under <code>.opencode/plugins/</code> are loaded automatically, like the local example above. To load published packages
or plugin directories from other locations, add them to <code>plugins</code> in <code>opencode.json(c)</code>:</p>

<h2 id="lifecycle">Lifecycle</h2>
<p><code>setup</code> runs when the plugin loads. It may return a cleanup function that runs when the plugin unloads.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Plugin } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;example&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ctx</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">`loaded in OpenCode ${</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">ctx</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">.</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">app</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">.</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">version</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">}`</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;unloaded&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="context">Context</h3>
<p>The plugin context is essentially an <a href="/v2/docs/build/client">OpenCode server client</a>.
Its read and action methods use the same inputs and responses as the client. It
adds plugin-only methods for transforms, runtime hooks, reloads, registrations,
and plugin options.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ctx.app.version)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<p><code>ctx.location</code> describes the location where this plugin instance is loaded. It
includes <code>directory</code>, optional <code>workspaceID</code>, and <code>project</code> metadata (<code>id</code>,
<code>directory</code>, and <code>canonical</code>). It is available in both Promise and Effect plugins.
This is the plugin instanceâs location, not the location of every session it can
access or event it receives.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ctx.location.directory)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ctx.location.project.canonical)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="options">Options</h3>
<p>Pass plugin options with the object form in <code>opencode.json(c)</code>.</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">opencode.jsonc</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="jsonc"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;plugins&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">      &quot;package&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;./plugins/company&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">      &quot;options&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">        &quot;strict&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Read those values from <code>ctx.options</code> during <code>setup</code>.</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">plugins/company/index.ts</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Plugin } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;company&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ctx</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> strict</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.options.strict </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="transforms">Transforms</h2>
<p>Transforms are synchronous edits to OpenCodeâs domain state, and each builds on earlier registrations in its domain.
Registry reads such as <code>ctx.model.list()</code> reflect every registration so far, including during plugin setup;
startup batching only coalesces update notifications and never delays what a read returns. Resource status APIs still
report the state of running resources: a registered definition does not mean its connection or other resource work has
completed.</p>
<p>Sources contribute providers and immutable model definitions through <code>ctx.provider.transform</code>.</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">plugins/models/index.ts</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Model, Plugin, Provider } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;company.models&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  async</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ctx</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> providerID</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Provider.</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">ID</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">make</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> models</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [{ </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">Model.Info.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(providerID, Model.</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">ID</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">make</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;reasoner&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)), name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Acme Reasoner&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        info: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">          ...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">Provider.Info.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">empty</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(providerID),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">          name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">          activation: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;enabled&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">          package: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;@opencode/ai/providers/openai-compatible&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">          settings: { baseURL: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        models,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> available</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Any registration, removal, or <code>reload()</code> marks the registry changed; the next read rebuilds it by replaying every
active transform in registration order onto a fresh value. Keep transforms cheap and repeatable. A value you have
already read is never modified by later rebuilds.</p>
<p>After provider transforms and availability are resolved, OpenCode materializes the active providersâ models and replays
every model transform in order. A model policy can inspect the whole candidate collection, including newly refreshed
sources.</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">plugins/model-budget/index.ts</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Plugin } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;company.model-budget&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  async</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ctx</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      editor</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        .</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        .</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">filter</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">model</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> model.cost.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">some</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">tier</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> tier.output </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&gt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 20</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        .</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">forEach</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">model</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">          editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(model.providerID, model.id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Captured inputs are not watched automatically. Load external data before the synchronous callback, then call
<code>reload()</code> after those inputs change. Here, <code>loadFromSource()</code> returns
<code>{ info: Provider.Info, models: readonly Model.Info[] }</code> entries.</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">plugins/models/index.ts</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Plugin } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> source</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { providers: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> loadFromSource</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">() }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> refresh</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> timer</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> setInterval</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(() </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> void</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> refresh</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">().</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">catch</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(console.error), </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">60_000</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> clearInterval</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(timer)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Provider changes invalidate the whole active model result, so the output-price policy still filters the refreshed models.
Use <code>ctx.model.reload()</code> when only an input captured by a model transform changes.</p>
<h2 id="api">API</h2>
<h3 id="agent">Agent</h3>
<p>Read all agents or fetch one by ID.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> agents</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.agent.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> build</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.agent.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ agentID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;build&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Register a transform to inspect, update, remove, or select the default agent.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.agent.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> agents</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> build</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;build&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;build&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;build&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">agent</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    agent.description </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;Builds features and fixes bugs&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;legacy&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Reload agents after external state used by a transform changes.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.agent.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>

<h3 id="providers">Providers</h3>
<p>Read available providers or inspect one providerâs metadata by ID.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> providers</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> provider</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Provider transforms edit provider settings and source definitions. Editor reads include inactive providers and expose
immutable definitions; <code>models.update</code> edits an owned copy of a source model.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">provider</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    provider.headers </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">provider.headers, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;x-company&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;engineering&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.models.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;claude-sonnet-5-5&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">model</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    model.name </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;Team Sonnet&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.models.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;legacy-model&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;legacy-provider&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Replace a providerâs source inventory with <code>models.set</code>, then reload after the captured definitions change.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> source</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { models: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> loadModels</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">() }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.models.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, source.models)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">source.models </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> loadModels</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>For account-specific discovery, pass the connection used to load the inventory as <code>sourceConnection</code>. OpenCode excludes
that provider while another connection is selected, until your plugin publishes its refreshed inventory.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> connection</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.connection.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">active</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">!</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">connection) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> inventory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> loadInventory</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(connection)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ info: inventory.provider, models: inventory.models, sourceConnection: connection })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="models">Models</h3>
<p>Read available models and the default selection.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> models</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> selected</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Model transforms edit the entire active-provider candidate collection. Disabled candidates remain editable until all
transforms finish; <code>update</code> can add a model only under an available provider.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">).</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">forEach</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">model</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(model.providerID, model.id, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">draft</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      draft.enabled </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> draft.capabilities.tools</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.default.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;claude-sonnet-5-5&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;legacy-model&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Callbacks edit raw model overrides. Provider defaults are merged once when OpenCode commits the result; unchanged reads
reuse that result. <code>editor.provider</code> exposes immutable source definitions, including inactive templates, rather than
model-transform output.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> source</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.provider.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)?.models.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;claude-sonnet-5-5&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">!</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">source) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, source.id, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">model</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    model.limit.context </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Math.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">min</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(model.limit.context, source.limit.context)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.model.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>

<h3 id="commands">Commands</h3>
<p>Read the commands available at a location.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> commands</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.command.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Register commands with a transform. The executor receives the session, prompt attachments, and requested delivery mode.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.command.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;security-review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    description: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review changes for security issues&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    execute</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ({ </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">sessionID</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">prompt</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">delivery</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">prompt</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        ...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">prompt,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        sessionID,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        text: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">`Review these changes for security issues.</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">\n\n</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">${</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">prompt</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">.</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">text</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">}`</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        delivery,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>

<h3 id="integrations">Integrations</h3>
<p>Read integrations and inspect active credentials.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> integrations</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> github</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ integrationID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;github&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> connection</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.connection.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">active</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;github&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>

<p>Connect integrations with an API key.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.connect.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">key</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.oauth.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">complete</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ integrationID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;github&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, attemptID: attempt.data.attemptID, code })</span></span>

<p>Command-based connections expose the same start, status, and cancel flow.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> attempt</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.integration.command.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">connect</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ integrationID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, methodID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;cli&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>

<p>Register a transform to inspect integrations and manage their authentication methods.</p>

<p>Reload integrations after external state used by a transform changes.</p>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">  connection</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    active</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">integrationID</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ConnectionInfo</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    resolve</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">connection</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ConnectionInfo</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">CredentialValue</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> IntegrationEditor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">id</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> IntegrationRef</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> undefined</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">id</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">integration</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> IntegrationRef</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">id</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">  method</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">integrationID</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> readonly</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> IntegrationMethod</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">[]</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>

<h3 id="credentials">Credentials</h3>
<p>Make a saved credential the active one for its integration.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.credential.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">activate</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ credentialID })</span></span></code></pre>
<h4 id="reference-5">Reference</h4>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> CredentialContext</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>

<h3 id="mcp">MCP</h3>
<p>List MCP servers and their current connection state.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> servers</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.mcp.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Plugins manage MCP servers only through transforms. Use <code>editor.set</code> to add or replace a server, <code>editor.update</code> to
change its configuration, and <code>editor.remove</code> to remove it. Inspect configuration with <code>editor.list</code> and <code>editor.get</code>.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.mcp.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> servers</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> docs</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;docs&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;docs&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, { type: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;remote&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, url: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;https://mcp.example.com&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;docs&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">server</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    server.disabled </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> false</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;legacy&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Set <code>disabled: true</code> in a transform to disable a server and disconnect it, or <code>disabled: false</code> to enable it and allow
OpenCode to connect. OpenCode reconciles server lifecycle from the transformed configuration.</p>
<p>Call <code>reload()</code> after external state used by a transform changes to reapply transforms and reconcile the servers.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.mcp.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
