<h3 id="generate">Generate</h3>
<p>Generate text with a selected model without creating a session, invoking tools, or adding to session history.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> review</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.generate.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">text</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  model: { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;claude-sonnet-5-5&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  prompt: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review this proposed action.&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="permissions">Permissions</h3>
<p>Ask for permission before performing an action. <code>assert</code> evaluates the sessionâs <a href="/v2/docs/permissions">permission rules</a>,
including saved approvals, and prompts the user when a rule resolves to <code>ask</code>. It resolves once the action is allowed.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">execute</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">input</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">context</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.permission.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">assert</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    sessionID: context.sessionID,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    agent: context.agent,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    source: { type: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;tool&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, messageID: context.messageID, id: context.id },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    action: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;deploy&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    resources: [input.environment],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    save: [input.environment],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { content: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> deploy</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(input.environment) }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<ul>
<li><code>action</code> and <code>resources</code> are matched against the rules. Any <code>deny</code> blocks the action; otherwise any <code>ask</code> prompts.</li>
<li><code>save</code> lists the patterns stored when the user chooses to always allow.</li>
<li><code>agent</code> selects whose rules apply and defaults to the sessionâs agent. <code>source</code> attaches the prompt to a tool call.</li>
<li><code>metadata</code> passes extra detail to permission prompts and <code>evaluate</code> hooks.</li>
</ul>
<p>A deny rule or a rejection with feedback rejects with a <code>Tool.Error</code> that carries the reason, so the model sees it and
continues. A plain rejection ends the current step. Let these errors propagate rather than returning them as tool
output.</p>
<p>Inspect or resolve pending permission requests.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> pending</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.permission.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> request</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.permission.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, requestID })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.permission.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reply</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, requestID, decision: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;once&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Replace the session-scoped permission rules. They are evaluated after the agentâs rules, and the
last matching rule wins. Child sessions inherit the rules in effect when they are created.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  sessionID,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  permissions: [{ action: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;edit&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, resource: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;/path/to/original/checkout/**&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, effect: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;deny&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="sessions">Sessions</h3>
<p>Create or read a session.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> created</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">create</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ title: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> session</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> messages</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">context</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>List sessions a page at a time. To get the next page, pass <code>cursor.next</code> back as <code>cursor</code>. The cursor remembers your
filters, so you donât need to repeat them.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> page</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ directory: ctx.location.directory, limit: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">20</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> next</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> page.cursor.next </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ cursor: page.cursor.next, limit: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">20</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> undefined</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Change the agent or model used by subsequent requests.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">switchAgent</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, agent: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;build&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">switchModel</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, model: { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;claude-sonnet-5-5&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> } })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Send user prompts, transient generation requests, commands, or synthetic messages.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> prompt</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">prompt</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, text: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review the current changes&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> generated</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">generate</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, prompt: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Summarize this project&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">command</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, text: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;--staged&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> synthetic</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">synthetic</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, text: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Deployment completed&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Rename, interrupt, or wait for a session.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, title: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">interrupt</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID, resume: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">false</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">wait</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Remove a temporary session with <code>remove</code>. Removal stops active execution and recursively deletes child sessions.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Request compaction with <code>compact</code>. It returns the admitted inbox item, not a completed summary; compaction runs at
the next step boundary by default. Pass <code>delivery: &quot;queue&quot;</code> to preserve queue order instead.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> compaction</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">compact</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID })</span></span></code></pre>

<a href="/v2/docs/api#schema-Session.Inbox.User"><code>Session.Inbox.User</code></a>, <a href="/v2/docs/api#schema-Session.Inbox.Synthetic"><code>Session.Inbox.Synthetic</code></a></p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionContext</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>

<h3 id="skills">Skills</h3>
<p>Read the skills available at a location.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> skills</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.skill.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Register a transform to inspect, add, update, or remove skills. <code>get(id)</code> returns the current editor entry, or <code>undefined</code>
when the skill is absent.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Skill } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.skill.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> skills</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    id: Skill.</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">ID</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">make</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    name: Skill.Name.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">make</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    description: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review the current changes&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    path: Skill.Info.fields.path.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">make</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;/workspace/.opencode/skills/review/SKILL.md&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    content: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Review the current changes for correctness and missing tests.&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>

<h3 id="tools">Tools</h3>
<p>Read the tools currently registered, after every transform, outside of a transform callback. Each tool has an <code>id</code>
containing its effective name.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> tools</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> read</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> tools.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">find</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">tool</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> tool.id </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;read&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Register, update, and remove tools with a synchronous transform, including in Promise plugins. Load external data
before registering or reloading. A later valid registration overrides the same effective tool name.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> registration</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">namespace</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    description: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Customer account tools&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;greeting&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    description: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Create a greeting&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    input: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      type: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;object&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      properties: { name: { type: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;string&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> } },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      required: [</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;name&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      additionalProperties: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">false</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    options: { namespace: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, codemode: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    execute</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">input</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">context</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> context.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">progress</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ status: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;greeting&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { content: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">`Hello ${</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">(</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">input</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> as</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> { </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">name</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> }).</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">name</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">}!`</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Promise tool executors receive <code>context.signal</code>. Pass it to cancellable work such as <code>fetch</code> so stopping the Session
also stops the underlying operation.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">execute</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ({ </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">url</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">context</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> response</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fetch</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(url, { signal: context.signal })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { content: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> response.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">text</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">() }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Call <code>reload()</code> after changing source data captured by the callback. Reload replays the active transforms without
changing their order; it does not rerun plugin setup.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Use <code>list()</code> and <code>get()</code> to inspect tools currently in the editor. Tools have an <code>id</code> containing their effective name,
and <code>get()</code> returns <code>undefined</code> when that ID is not present. Use <code>update</code> and <code>remove</code> with the effective tool name,
including its namespace (<code>acme_greeting</code> above). Dots in
namespaces and unsupported characters in tool names become <code>_</code>. Missing names are ignored; creating a tool requires
<code>add</code> with a complete definition. Updates preserve the name and namespace. Assign new schemas or options to replace
them rather than mutating nested values. Invalid updates are logged and leave the previous definition intact.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme_greeting&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">tool</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    tool.description </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;Greet the user by name&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;acme_obsolete&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Updates and removals replay in order with additions, including after MCP catalog refreshes. Disposing their
registration removes those changes and rebuilds from the remaining transforms.</p>
<p>Dispose a registration to remove its transform and rebuild from the remaining transforms, revealing any earlier
definition it overrode. Disposal is idempotent, and unloading the plugin also disposes its registrations.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> registration.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">dispose</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Each model request captures a stable, executable tool snapshot. Later transforms, reloads, and disposal affect future
snapshots, not the definitions, Code Mode namespace descriptions, or executors already captured. Executors that close
over mutable plugin data still observe that data; capture a value inside the transform when it must remain tied to
that definition.</p>

<a href="/v2/docs/api#schema-Tool.FileContent"><code>Tool.FileContent</code></a></p>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolEditor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  namespace</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">namespace</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">name</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">description</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">tool</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolInfo</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  update</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">id</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">update</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">tool</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Types</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Mutable</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ToolInfo</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">id</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="vcs">VCS</h3>
<p>Read repository information, working-copy status, or file diffs.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> info</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.vcs.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> branches</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.vcs.branch.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ search: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;feature&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, limit: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">10</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> changes</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.vcs.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">status</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> diff</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.vcs.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">diff</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ mode: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;working&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, context: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Register a location-scoped VCS provider with a transform. A provider matching the detected repository type is selected
automatically; use <code>editor.default.set</code> to select a different provider.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.vcs.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;custom&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Custom VCS&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    info</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ({ branch: { current: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;feature&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, default: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;main&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> } }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    branches</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">input</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> readBranches</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(input, signal),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    status</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">scope</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> readStatus</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(scope.worktree, signal),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    diff</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">input</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> readDiff</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(input, signal),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.default.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;custom&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Provider callbacks receive the current location, working-copy root, canonical project root, and optional repository
store. Diff callbacks also receive the selected mode, requested context, and output byte budget. Repository discovery
continues to use OpenCodeâs built-in Git and Mercurial detectors.</p>
<p>Providers can optionally implement <code>init(scope, { signal })</code> to create a repository in <code>scope.worktree</code>. Initialization
selects the provider by ID (Git by default), not by the detected repository type. Register a provider with <code>init</code> even
when the location has no repository yet; OpenCode checks that the directory exists and has no repository before calling
it, then rediscovers the project. New repository formats still need discovery support beyond this callback.</p>

<a href="/v2/docs/api#schema-FileDiff.Info"><code>FileDiff.Info</code></a></p>
