<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">providerID</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> VcsDefinition</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  init</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">scope</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> VcsScope</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">context</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AbortSignal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D">  // Existing info, branches, status, and diff callbacks are required.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="worktrees">Worktrees</h3>
<p>Register a local worktree strategy using the normal plugin lifecycle. The implementation module in this example owns
option validation and the backendâs <code>create</code>, <code>remove</code>, and <code>list</code> operations.</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">plugins/worktrees/index.ts</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Plugin } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { makeStrategy } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;./strategy&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;company.worktrees&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  async</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ctx</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> strategy</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> makeStrategy</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(ctx.options)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(strategy)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Load the plugin through <code>plugins</code> and configure the common destination separately:</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">opencode.jsonc</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="jsonc"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;worktree&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: { </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">&quot;directory&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;../worktrees&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;plugins&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: [{ </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">&quot;package&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;./plugins/worktrees&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">&quot;options&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: {} }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Adding a strategy selects it automatically. The last active registration wins; no strategy-selection config is needed.</li>
<li>Disposing its registration or unloading the plugin restores the previous implementation, ultimately the bundled Git strategy.</li>
<li><code>reload()</code> replays transforms after captured inputs change; it does not rerun plugin setup.</li>
<li>Creation failures do not retry through Git. Existing worktrees retain their recorded owner even when the default changes.</li>
<li>Strategies manage local directories. Remote workspace provisioning is not part of this interface.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="operations">Operations</h4>
<p>The plugin context exposes the same project-based worktree operations as the client. Every operation requires a
<code>projectID</code>. <code>list</code> reads only saved inventory, including known checkouts of the same project; it does not load
configuration, start plugins, or discover filesystem changes.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> projectID</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.location.project.id</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> created</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">create</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ projectID, name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;task&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> inventory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ projectID })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">refresh</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ projectID })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ projectID, directory: created.directory, force: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">false</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Create loads configuration and plugins from the projectâs saved <code>canonical</code> directory and uses their selected strategy.
It accepts an optional parent <code>directory</code>, source <code>from</code>, starting <code>branch</code>, and <code>name</code>.
The source defaults to the canonical checkout, and a <code>from</code> override must belong to that project. Choosing a source
does not change the configuration root.
A supplied starting ref must be supported by the selected strategy;
Riftâs native snapshot operation, for example, has no ref-selection option.</p>
<p>Create and refresh wait for the canonical checkoutâs plugins to activate. Calls made during that checkoutâs plugin
setup see registrations made so far and do not wait for their own activation.</p>
<p>Remove loads the canonical configuration and activates its plugins, then uses the worktreeâs recorded strategy rather
than the currently selected default. An unavailable recorded owner produces an error. The worktreeâs destination can
be elsewhere:</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  directory: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;/worktrees/task&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  projectID,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  force: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">false</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Missing owners fail removal rather than falling back to Git. A strategy can throw <code>new Worktree.OperationError({ message: &quot;Uncommitted changes&quot;, forceRequired: true })</code>, importing <code>Worktree</code> from <code>@opencode/plugin</code>, to request
force confirmation without depending on Core or Git errors.</p>
<p>Refresh loads canonical configuration and asks all available strategies to find worktrees across known checkout roots.
It combines results, preserves recorded ownership, and prunes missing directories. A failed discovery source is logged
while other sources continue. Existing directories remain saved even if no available strategy reports them. Observe
<code>worktree.updated</code> and call <code>list</code> again to read changed inventory.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.worktree.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">refresh</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ projectID })</span></span></code></pre>
<h4 id="reference-14">Reference</h4>
<p>Implementations receive a suggested destination after naming and collision handling. Return the actual directory from
<code>create</code>; it may differ when a backend requires its own layout. OpenCode resolves the returned path and uses it for
inventory, startup commands, and the API result. The returned directory must exist.</p>
<p>Strategies choosing another destination handle naming collisions there. OpenCode still creates the suggested parent
directory before calling the strategy. <code>list</code> must report only directories the strategy owns, plus any repository roots.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> WorktreeDefinition</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  readonly</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> id</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  create</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">    input</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">sourceDirectory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">directory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">branch</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">    context</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AbortSignal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  )</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;{ </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">directory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  remove</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">input</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">directory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">force</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> boolean</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }, </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">context</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AbortSignal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  list</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">    sourceDirectory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">    context</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AbortSignal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  )</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">readonly</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">directory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">type</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;root&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;worktree&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }[]&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> WorktreeEditor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">definition</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> WorktreeDefinition</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> WorktreeDomain</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> extends</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> WorktreeApi</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">callback</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> WorktreeEditor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Registration</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Promise callbacks must cooperate with <code>signal</code> cancellation. Effect callbacks return Effects and use Effect interruption
instead. Registrations remain scoped to the plugin runtime; project operations use the canonical checkoutâs registry.
Core configuration adapters feed directory settings into that registry.</p>
<h3 id="websearch">Websearch</h3>
<p>List websearch providers or run a query through the selected provider.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> providers</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.websearch.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">providers</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> results</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.websearch.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">query</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ query: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;OpenCode plugins&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;internal&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Register a provider and select the default provider with a transform.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.websearch.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;internal&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    name: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Internal search&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    execute</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ({ </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">query</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }, { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> response</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fetch</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">`https://search.example.com?q=${</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">encodeURIComponent</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">(</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">query</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">)</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">}`</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, { signal })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> result</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> response.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">json</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">url</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">title</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">; </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">content</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [{ url: result.url, title: result.title, content: result.content, time: {} }]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  editor.default.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;internal&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Disable websearch by selecting <code>false</code>, or reload providers after external state changes.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.websearch.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">transform</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">editor</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> editor.default.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">false</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.websearch.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">reload</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>

<h3 id="events">Events</h3>
<p>Subscribe to the connected serverâs public event stream. Abort the stream during plugin cleanup.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> controller</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> new</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AbortController</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  for</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> event</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> of</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.event.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">subscribe</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ signal: controller.signal })) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.type)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> controller.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">abort</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<h4 id="reference-16">Reference</h4>
<p>Schema: <a href="/v2/docs/api#schema-V2EventEncoded"><code>V2EventEncoded</code></a></p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> EventContext</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  subscribe</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">options</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">signal</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AbortSignal</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> AsyncIterable</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">OpenCodeEvent</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<h2 id="hooks">Hooks</h2>
<p>Hooks intercept live operations. Register a hook on its domain and dispose the
returned registration when it is no longer needed.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> registration</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;context&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {})</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> registration.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">dispose</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Multiple plugins can register the same hook. OpenCode runs them in plugin order,
so later hooks see changes made by earlier hooks.</p>
<h3 id="sessions-1">Sessions</h3>
<p>Session hooks intercept prompt admission, model calls, native HTTP, and retries.</p>
<h4 id="prompt-admission">Prompt admission</h4>
<p>Intercept incoming user prompts before attachment and skill resolution and durable inbox admission:</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;prompt&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.prompt.text </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> event.prompt.text.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">replaceAll</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;company-secret&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;[redacted]&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.prompt.files </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">??=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> []</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.prompt.files.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ uri: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;file:///project/policy.md&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.metadata </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">event.metadata, source: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;company-policy&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.delivery </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;queue&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>The hook receives an owned, mutable draft:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>prompt</code> contains <code>text</code>, <code>files</code>, agent mentions in <code>agents</code>, and selected <code>skills</code>.</li>
<li><code>metadata</code> contains application-defined admission metadata.</li>
<li><code>delivery</code> is <code>&quot;steer&quot;</code> by default and can be changed to <code>&quot;queue&quot;</code>.</li>
<li>Session and message IDs are readonly; a hook cannot redirect admission.</li>
</ul>
<p>Files and skills added by a hook follow the normal resolution and validation
path. When rewriting text, update or remove attachment <code>mention</code> offsets that no
longer match. Agent mentions do not switch the sessionâs active agent.</p>
<p>Prompt hooks run under these conditions:</p>
<ul>
<li>Hooks run in registration order after the sessionâs location plugins are ready.</li>
<li>Commands that submit through <code>session.prompt</code> run the hook.</li>
<li>Synthetic messages, shell messages, compaction controls, and move controls do not.</li>
<li>The hook runs once during admission, not before every model call.</li>
<li>Provider scoping is unavailable because model resolution happens after admission.</li>
</ul>
<p>Edits become the canonical persisted user input. If preparation fails or is
interrupted, OpenCode does not admit the prompt.</p>
<p>Keep prompt hooks retry-safe. They are not an exactly-once side-effect boundary:</p>
<ul>
<li>Retrying an ID already pending or delivered returns the original admission without rerunning hooks.</li>
<li>Concurrent submissions can run hooks more than once, but only the first successful admission wins.</li>
<li>Prompt hooks transform input and do not expose a typed rejection API.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="model-requests">Model requests</h4>
<p>Modify assembled system instructions, messages, tools, or request options immediately before model dispatch. Each
kind of request a session issues has its own hook, so a plugin can treat the agent loop and auxiliary requests
differently:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>context</code> runs for the agent loop, including tool-driven continuations.</li>
<li><code>compaction</code> runs for checkpoint summaries. <code>messages</code> is the transcript being summarized; OpenCode appends its
summary prompt after hooks run. Set <code>result</code> to record the compaction yourself and skip the model call; it takes the
same fields as a completed compaction message.</li>
<li><code>generate</code> runs for transient <code>ctx.session.generate</code> calls.</li>
<li><code>title</code> runs for title generation. It has no <code>agent</code> or <code>tools</code>. Set <code>result</code> to supply the title yourself.</li>
</ul>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;context&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.system.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ type: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;text&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, text: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;Keep the review focused on correctness.&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  delete</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> event.tools.write</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.options.temperature </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.2</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.options.maxTokens </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 8_000</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;compaction&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.result </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { summary: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> summarize</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.messages) }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Changes affect only the outgoing model call, not persisted history or configuration. A hook that should apply to
every request must register for each kind.</p>
<p>Request overrides follow these rules:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>options</code> starts empty for each model call; it does not contain resolved model settings.</li>
<li>Typed keys are generation settings; any other key is passed to the selected protocol as a provider option.</li>
<li>Hooks run in registration order and see overrides made by earlier hooks.</li>
<li>Request overrides take precedence over model defaults, which take precedence over route defaults.</li>
<li>Provider option objects merge recursively; arrays and scalar values replace earlier values.</li>
<li>Deleting an override or setting it to <code>undefined</code> falls back to configured defaults rather than removing them.</li>
<li>Raw HTTP body overlays apply after protocol lowering and can override the resulting fields.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="provider-options">Provider options</h4>
<p>Provider options use the selected protocolâs semantic option names, not raw HTTP body fields. Scope provider-specific
settings to the matching provider. For example, OpenAI Responses uses <code>reasoningEffort</code>:</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;context&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    event.options.reasoningEffort </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;high&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;openai&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Generation options depend on the selected protocol and model:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>maxTokens</code> is the semantic output-token limit.</li>
<li>Gemini supports <code>topK</code>; OpenAI Responses does not expose it.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="model-request">Model request</h4>
<p>Modify model request settings and optionally scope the hook to one provider. The event carries the same <code>kind</code>
as the HTTP hooks below.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;model.request&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    event.headers[</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;x-plugin&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;review&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span></code></pre>
<h4 id="native-http">Native HTTP</h4>
<p>Modify native provider requests or responses. Their bodies are one-shot streams; clone or replace a body before reading
it.</p>
<p>Both hooks run for every request a session issues. <code>event.kind</code> says which flow issued it: <code>&quot;primary&quot;</code> for the agent
loop, <code>&quot;compaction&quot;</code> for checkpoint summaries, <code>&quot;title&quot;</code> for title generation, and <code>&quot;generate&quot;</code> for transient
<code>ctx.session.generate</code> calls. Use it instead of the agent ID to tell auxiliary requests apart.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;http.request&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.request.headers.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;x-session-id&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, event.sessionID)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.kind </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;title&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) event.request.headers.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;x-priority&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;background&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;http.response&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.response </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> new</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Response</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.response.body, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    status: event.response.status,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    headers: { </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">Object.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fromEntries</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.response.headers), </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;x-plugin&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;review&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<h4 id="native-websocket-experimental">Native WebSocket (experimental)</h4>
<p>Providers that stream over a WebSocket open one connection per session and reuse it across requests, so
<code>http.request</code> and <code>http.response</code> never see that traffic. The <code>experimental.ws.handshake</code> hook runs once per model
call before the connection is selected. It carries the same <code>sessionID</code>, <code>agent</code>, <code>model</code>, and <code>kind</code> as the HTTP
hooks, plus the connection <code>url</code> and <code>headers</code>. Changing <code>url</code> or <code>headers</code> changes which socket the request uses, so a
rotated bearer token reopens the connection.</p>
<p>HTTP hooks still run for every HTTP request, including the request a WebSocket route falls back to.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;experimental.ws.handshake&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    event.headers.authorization </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> `Bearer ${</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> mintToken</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">(</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">event</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">.</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">url</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">)</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">}`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    delete</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> event.headers[</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;api-key&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;azure&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span></code></pre>
<p><code>experimental.ws.send</code> and <code>experimental.ws.receive</code> expose the frames themselves, the WebSocket counterpart of
editing an HTTP request or response body. <code>send</code> runs after the provider driver builds an outbound frame and before it
is written; <code>receive</code> runs on each inbound frame before the driver observes it. Both carry the frame as a string and
send whatever <code>frame</code> holds when the hook returns.</p>
<p>OpenCode does not validate rewritten frames. The driver tracks state from the providerâs replies, so a rewrite that
changes protocol meaning is the pluginâs responsibility, just as a rewritten HTTP body is.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;experimental.ws.send&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> body</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> JSON</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">parse</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.frame)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (body.type </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;response.create&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) body.metadata </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">body.metadata, session: event.sessionID }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    event.frame </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> JSON</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">stringify</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(body)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;openai&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;experimental.ws.receive&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.frame.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">includes</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&#39;&quot;type&quot;:&quot;error&quot;&#39;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)) console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">error</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.frame)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>These hooks are experimental and their names or shapes may change.</p>
<h4 id="retry-policy">Retry policy</h4>
<p>Override the retry decision for a provider failure or replace its delay in milliseconds. The hook runs after OpenCode
classifies the failure and proposes its policy, but before any retry is scheduled. It does not expose how OpenCode
internally performs the next attempt.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;retry&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.error.status </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 429</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    event.decision </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { retry: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, delay: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">10_000</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.error.type </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;provider.invalid-request&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &amp;&amp;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> event.attempt </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    event.decision </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { retry: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, delay: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.attempt </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&gt;=</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 3</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) event.decision </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { retry: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">false</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Retry hooks can change OpenCodeâs initial <code>decision</code> by making a terminal failure
retryable or vetoing a proposed retry.</p>
<p>Retry decisions follow these rules:</p>
<ul>
<li>Multiple hooks run in registration order, and later hooks see the current decision.</li>
<li>The built-in maximum attempt count remains a hard limit.</li>
<li><code>attempt</code> is the physical attempt under consideration. The initial request is <code>1</code>, so the first retry is <code>2</code>.</li>
<li>Invalid delays such as <code>NaN</code>, infinity, or negative values fall back to the computed delay.</li>
<li>Context-overflow recovery is separate because it compacts the conversation instead of retrying the same request.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="session-reference">Session reference</h4>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> type</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { SessionPrompt } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin/promise/session&quot;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;model.request&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionModelRequestHook</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;http.request&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionHttpRequestHook</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;http.response&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionHttpResponseHook</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;experimental.ws.handshake&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionWebSocketHandshakeHook</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionWebSocketHandshakeHook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
