<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">  metadata</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Record</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">unknown</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">  tokens</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> TokenUsage</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionHookContext</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Name</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> extends</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> keyof</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionHooks</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">    name</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Name</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    callback</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> SessionHooks</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">[</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Name</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt; </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">|</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> void</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">    options</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Name</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> extends</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;prompt&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> ?</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> never</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> :</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">providerID</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?:</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> string</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  )</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Promise</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Registration</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre>
<h3 id="permissions-1">Permissions</h3>
<p>Review permission decisions after configured rules are evaluated and before an
action runs or a permission prompt is published.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.permission.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;evaluate&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.action </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;read&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">return</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> messages</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.session.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">context</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ sessionID: event.sessionID })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> review</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.generate.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">text</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    model: { providerID: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;anthropic&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;claude-sonnet-5-5&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    prompt: </span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">buildSafetyPrompt</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({ messages, action: event.action, resources: event.resources }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  const</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> decision</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> parseDecision</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(review.text)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.effect </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> decision.effect</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.message </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> decision.reason</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Permission hooks follow these rules:</p>
<ul>
<li>Hooks run for <code>allow</code> and <code>ask</code> decisions.</li>
<li>An explicit configured <code>deny</code> is final and does not invoke the hook.</li>
<li>A hook may change <code>effect</code> to <code>allow</code>, <code>ask</code>, or <code>deny</code>.</li>
<li><code>message</code> appears in an escalated permission request or becomes the denial reason.</li>
</ul>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">  effect</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;allow&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;ask&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;deny&quot;</span></span>

<h3 id="shell">Shell</h3>
<p>Modify shell commands, working directories, timeouts, executables, or environment variables before execution.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.shell.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;create.before&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.timeout </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> Math.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">min</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.timeout, </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">60_000</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  event.env.</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">COMPANY_ENV</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;development&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>

<h3 id="tools-1">Tools</h3>
<p>Inspect or replace tool input before execution.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;execute.before&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.tool </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;read&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.input)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<p>Inspect successful results or failures after execution.</p>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;execute.after&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, (</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">event</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.status </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;completed&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) event.result </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">event.result, metadata: { observed: </span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> } }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  if</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (event.status </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">===</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;error&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">error</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(event.error.message)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">})</span></span></code></pre>
<h4 id="reference-19">Reference</h4>
<pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">interface</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolHooks</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;execute.before&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolExecuteBefore</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  &quot;execute.after&quot;</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolExecuteCompleted</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolExecuteFailed</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">  hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&lt;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Name</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> extends</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> keyof</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ToolHooks</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&gt;(</span></span>

<h2 id="publish">Publish</h2>
<p>A package plugin uses the same default export as a local plugin. A minimal
manifest is:</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">package.json</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="json"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;name&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;opencode-acme-plugin&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;version&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;1.0.0&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;module&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;exports&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    &quot;.&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;./src/index.ts&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    &quot;./rpc&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;./src/rpc.ts&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  &quot;dependencies&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;latest&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>The <code>./rpc</code> export is optional; include it when publishing a shared
<a href="/v2/docs/build/plugins/rpc">RPC contract</a> for other plugins and clients to import
without loading your implementation.</p>
<p>Use versions compatible with the OpenCode release you target and test the
installed package, not only a workspace-linked copy. Publish a compatible
plugin update when you adopt a newer API contract.</p>
<h2 id="support-v1">Support V1</h2>
<p>A plugin can support V1 and V2 from the same package entrypoint. Default export
one object with a V1 <code>server()</code> function and a V2 <code>setup()</code> function:</p>
<figure class="astro-code-figure"><figcaption class="astro-code-title">src/index.ts</figcaption><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;overflow-x:auto" tabindex="0" data-language="ts"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> { Plugin } </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> &quot;@opencode/plugin&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">export</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> default</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  ...</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">Plugin.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">define</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    id: </span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;example&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    async</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> setup</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">ctx</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">      await</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ctx.tool.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">hook</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;execute.before&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;A tool is about to run&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">  async</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> server</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">      &quot;tool.execute.before&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">: </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">async</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> () </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        console.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">log</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">&quot;A tool is about to run&quot;</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">      },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>V1 calls <code>server()</code> and uses the returned hooks.</li>
<li>V2 reads the default exportâs <code>id</code> and <code>setup()</code> (or <code>effect()</code> for Effect plugins), ignoring <code>server()</code>.</li>
<li>Keep each implementation on its own API; sharing an export does not translate V1 hooks into V2 hooks.</li>
<li>Spread <code>Plugin.define(...)</code> into the exported object so it type-checks the V2 definition separately from <code>server()</code>.</li>
</ul>
<p>The V1 object form is supported in OpenCode <code>1.18.29</code>. Older V1 releases may
expect function exports instead; test the installed package with the oldest V1
release you intend to support and with V2.</p></article></main><aside class="toc-column"><nav class="docs-toc" aria-label="On this page"><h2>On this page</h2><ul><li><a href="#lifecycle">Lifecycle</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#context">Context</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#options">Options</a></li><li><a href="#transforms">Transforms</a></li><li><a href="#api">API</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#agent">Agent</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#providers">Providers</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#models">Models</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#commands">Commands</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#integrations">Integrations</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#credentials">Credentials</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#mcp">MCP</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#plugins">Plugins</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#references">References</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#generate">Generate</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#permissions">Permissions</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#sessions">Sessions</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#skills">Skills</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#storage">Storage</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#tools">Tools</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#vcs">VCS</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#worktrees">Worktrees</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#websearch">Websearch</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#events">Events</a></li><li><a href="#hooks">Hooks</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#sessions-1">Sessions</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#permissions-1">Permissions</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#shell">Shell</a></li><li class="nested"><a href="#tools-1">Tools</a></li><li><a href="#publish">Publish</a></li><li><a href="#support-v1">Support V1</a></li></ul></nav></aside></div><script>
